## Fix 31 — Safety guides: tab keyboard focus + read-aloud lifecycle (GDE)

- **Part:** Fix 31 (triage row 62) — 2 findings, both Minor, both in `src/components/SafetyGuides.tsx` (256 lines, the only file). Prep: `~/workspace/wildfire-fix-prep/fix31-prep.md` (base ba430bd; file byte-identical between ba430bd and main 7196aac, line numbers held). Branch: `fix/wildfire-31-safety-guides` from main 7196aac.
- **LIVE defects, not dormant:** `SafetyGuides` is mounted in `MainContent.tsx:305–309` whenever the user opens the guides app tab, and unmounts when they leave it — unlike Fix 30's dormant AICopilot, both findings are reachable in production.
- **GDE-1** (report line 159): `onTabKeyDown` updated `activeTab` only — focus never moved, so the next arrow press recomputed from the still-focused tab's id and selection could never advance past the second tab; focus ended on an inactive tab with `tabIndex=-1`. Fix: a refs array (`tabRefs`, indexed in the `map` order, same order as `tabIds`) + `tabRefs.current[nextIndex]?.focus()` after `setActiveTab`. The ref callback uses braces (returns `undefined` — React 19 treats a returned value as a cleanup fn). No ARIA changes (skeleton already correct); Home/End gain focus movement through the same path.
- **GDE-2** (report line 160): `readAloud` starts the global `speechSynthesis`; only another read-button click cancels. Switching app tabs unmounted the component and took the stop button with it while the utterance kept playing; switching guide tab/language replaced the panel text while the old utterance kept speaking beside it. Fix (no change to `readAloud` itself): (1) unmount cleanup effect — `window.speechSynthesis?.cancel()` (the SafeEvacuation:346 one-line pattern); (2) content-change effect on `[activeTab, lang]` with a `prevContentRef` guard so the mount run is a no-op (opening the guides tab must not cancel another component's speech) — on a real change it cancels and `setSpeaking(false)` (load-bearing: without it the button stays in its "speaking" state and the next click would cancel instead of starting a new read).
- **Decisions (all to the prep's recommendations):** D1 refs array; D2 keep the current ArrowRight→next mapping (RTL arrow mirroring is a real WAI-ARIA inconsistency but unrequested scope — recorded as a follow-up below, not a ride-along); D3 unconditional cancel on a real content change + unmount, with the `prevContentRef` guard only against the mount run.
- **Tests (8 new):** NEW `tests/components/safety-guides.test.tsx` — GDE-1: two Rights reach the third tab with focus; ArrowLeft wraps to the last tab with focus; Home/End with focus; click control. GDE-2: tab change stops speech + resets the toggle (second click speaks again); language change stops speech + next read uses fr-FR; unmount stops speech; double-click toggle control. Speech stubs copy the Fix 26b pattern; assertions on speak/cancel counts + utterance `lang`, never on jsdom `innerText`.
- **Fail-without-fix (stash of the single changed file, proven):** 8/8 pass with the fix; exactly the 6 non-control tests fail on old code (2 controls pass — correct).
- **Fix 31b (CodeRabbit review 6030686627 on PR #162, 2 Minors, both verified VALID):** (1) the unmount cleanup called `cancel()` unconditionally — under StrictMode (main.tsx:68) the simulated unmount runs it on mount, so opening the guides tab could cancel another component's speech before any guide read starts (and on a real unmount it would also stop e.g. TrappedSOSModal's `speakEmergency`, which has no unmount cancel); (2) `utterance.onend`/`onerror` reset `speaking` unconditionally — a cancelled utterance's late event can fire after a later `speak()`, flipping `speaking` false mid-utterance so the next click queues another utterance instead of stopping the active one. Fix (one mechanism): a `currentUtteranceRef` names the utterance this guide owns — set on `speak`, cleared on cancel; the unmount/content-change cancels run only while it is non-null, and each `onend`/`onerror` resets `speaking` only while its own utterance is still current. Tests: +2 — StrictMode mount cancels nothing; a late error from a superseded utterance does not flip the toggle (the late event MUST be invoked inside `act(...)` — a bare call schedules the state update but it never flushes before the next click's handler reads `speaking` from its closure, so the test passed vacuously on old code; proven by probe).
- **Fail-without-fix (31b, stash of the single changed file, proven):** 10/10 pass with the fix; exactly the 2 new 31b tests fail on old code (8 others pass — correct, 31b changes only the two edge cases).
- **Local gates (Fix 31 + 31b):** lint 0, build pass, server 920/920 (85 files), React 1197/1198 in the full run (108 files) — the single failure is the Fix 26 safe-evacuation Leaflet-init timing flake (untouched by 31/31b), which passed 8/8 on the second isolated re-run (it also flaked once in isolation under CPU pressure); all 10 new tests pass. e2e not runnable in this VM (Firebase emulator needs Java) — CI covers it.
- **Fix 31 MERGED 2026-11-24 ~00:43 CET — PR #162, merge 5da2047** (parents 7196aac + 1b8d459; branch NOT deleted). Tally now **86 merged / 193 remaining / 48 parts**. Fix 31 + 31b + 31c: 2 Minors (GDE-1, GDE-2) + 31b (speech cancels scoped to own utterance, reviews 6030686627) + 31c (comment correction, review 6030887055). Gates: lint 0, build pass, server 920/920, React 1197/1198 (1 known safe-evacuation flake, passes 8/8 on re-run).
- **Follow-up (not a blocker, D2):** RTL arrow mirroring — the wrapper sets `dir=rtl` in Arabic but the handler maps ArrowRight→next unconditionally; per the WAI-ARIA tabs pattern RTL should mirror the arrows. Deserves its own verification, not a ride-along on a focus fix.
- **Fix 31c (CodeRabbit review 6030887055 on PR #162 watch 2, 1 Minor, verified VALID in its premise):** the 31b comment overclaimed — the ref guard proves the guide owns an utterance, not that the queue contains only the guide's utterance, because `cancel()` is global. No minimal code fix exists inside SafetyGuides.tsx alone (the Web Speech API has no per-utterance cancel and no queue introspection; CodeRabbit itself labels it "Heavy lift" and proposes no patch). Decision (parent): correct the comment to state the actual guarantee and accept the residual — the scenario needs a guide read in progress AND TrappedSOSModal emergency speech queued behind it AND a guide tab/view change inside that window, and the SOS modal itself stays visible. Removing the cancels would reintroduce GDE-2, the finding this PR fixes. Change: comment-only (lines 82–88). Gates: lint 0 + safety-guides tests 10/10 re-run (comment-only change; full suites unchanged by it — last full run: server 920/920, React 1197/1198 with the known safe-evacuation flake).
- **Follow-up (not a blocker, from review 6030887055):** shared speech ownership/coordination across the three speaking components (SafetyGuides, SafeEvacuation, TrappedSOSModal) so one component's cancel can never stop another's queued utterance. Needs a design decision first; prep risk R2 had already analyzed the SOS overlap as contrived and accepted the house precedent.

## Fix 32 — Administration and command UI (ADM, CMD, AUD)

- **Part:** Fix 32 (triage row 63) — 5 findings, all Minor, all TypeScript/React, no server change. Prep: `~/workspace/wildfire-fix-prep/fix32-prep.md` (base 1710753; `AdminPanel.tsx`, `CentralCommand.tsx`, `AuditLog.tsx` byte-identical between 1710753 and main 5da2047, so prep line numbers held; `ConfirmDialog.tsx` differs only by Fix 30's DLG-1). Branch: `fix/wildfire-32-admin-command-audit` from main **5da2047** (PR #162 merge). All 8 open decisions D1–D8 resolved to the prep's recommendations.
- **Findings (all live, operator-facing):** **ADM-3** (`AdminPanel.tsx`) — logout flipped to the login surface before the revocation POST and swallowed failures, so a failed logout left a valid HTTP-only cookie behind and the next remount silently re-authenticated the workstation. **ADM-4** (`AdminPanel.tsx`) — `safePage` clamped only the rendered page; a deletion/refresh shrinking `totalPages` left the page state stale and Previous decremented the stale state (5 → 4 while the display stayed 3). **CMD-2** (`CentralCommand.tsx`) — the header Refresh called only `fetchUserLocations`; the SOS list and team roster are independent local state, so they stayed up to 15 s stale after a manual refresh. **CMD-3** (`CentralCommand.tsx`) — resolve rode a raw fetch (no timeout, no pending state) and the dialog closed before the request was awaited, so the same SOS could be confirmed again while the first resolve stalled. **AUD-1** (`AuditLog.tsx`) — load failures were silent, so a failed load rendered exactly like an empty trail ("No actions recorded").
- **Implemented:** ADM-3 — awaited `handleLogout` (rides `apiFetch`'s 15 s ceiling; switches to the login surface only on `res.ok`, error toast otherwise) + `loggingOut` pending flag (button disabled while in flight) + `logoutLocal()` for the two session-expiry paths (`handleAuthError`, moderation `onSessionExpiry`), which fire no logout POST. Boundary: a 200 does not guarantee server-side revocation — the route discards the `revokeAdminSession` boolean; that is **ADM-5, triaged to Fix 41 (server)**. ADM-4 — state-clamp effect on `[totalPages]` + Previous-callback clamp mirroring Next's shape; the effect cannot loop (identical-value `setPage` bails out). The derivation block (`adminReports` / `filteredReports` / `totalPages` / `safePage` / `paginatedReports`) moved above the early `authChecking`/`!isAuthenticated` returns — the effect is a hook and must run on every render; placing it after the returns crashed the suite with "rendered more hooks than during the previous render" (caught by the tests, fixed by the move, no behavior change). CMD-2 — new `refreshAll` callback calling the same trio the 15 s poll calls; button `onClick={refreshAll}`; no double parent refresh (only the locations loader calls `onRefresh`). CMD-3 — resolve rides `apiFetch` (15 s ceiling), `resolvingId` dedupes (cleared in `finally`), 401 routes to `setUnlocked(false)` like the neighbouring mutations, dialog closes on success only and stays open on failure for retry in place; new optional `confirmDisabled` prop on `ConfirmDialog` (default `false`, disabled styling, cancel stays live — trapping the operator mid-request is worse than a dismissed dialog). AUD-1 — `loadError` state (HTTP status in the message for non-OK, connectivity wording for transport failures); error-with-entries renders a banner above the list, error-without-entries renders the banner instead of the empty message; a successful retry clears it. Out of scope per D8: `fetchEntries` still rides a raw fetch with no timeout — recorded as a follow-up below.
- **Tests (18 new):** `tests/components/admin-panel.test.tsx` +6 (ADM-3: failed logout keeps the operator logged in + error toast; 500 keeps logged in; success switches + exactly one POST; expiry path fires no POST — via the delete 401 → `handleAuthError` path. ADM-4: shrink-then-Previous reaches the earlier page; Next-from-last control). `tests/components/central-command.test.tsx` +5 (CMD-2: manual Refresh calls all three loaders; parent refresh exactly once control. CMD-3: double-confirm issues one POST + dialog stays open while pending + confirm disabled; failed resolve keeps the dialog open for retry; resolve carries an `AbortSignal`). `tests/components/confirm-dialog.test.tsx` +2 (confirmDisabled disables confirm, cancel stays live; default enabled control). NEW `tests/components/audit-log.test.tsx` (5: 500 → banner not empty message; transport failure → banner; failed refresh keeps old entries under a banner; empty-trail control; successful retry clears the banner).
- **Test-design lesson (CMD-3):** the mount fetch returns `[]` for `/api/sos/full` in the base mock, which wipes the prop-initialized SOS list — the first CMD-3 run failed because the row vanished between the waitFor and the dialog click (only the dialog's "تم الإنقاذ" remained, so `getAllByText(...)[1]` was undefined). The CMD-3 tests stub `/api/sos/full` to return the SOS, like the Fix 24 harness gates its first fetch. The row must exist when the dialog opens.
- **Fail-without-fix (per-file stash, all proven):** AdminPanel stash → 4/6 fail (the 2 controls pass — successful logout, Next-stays-put — correct). CentralCommand + ConfirmDialog + AuditLog stash → 9/18 fail in the three files (CMD-2 refresh, CMD-3 ×3, confirmDisabled, AUD-1 ×3 + the retry-clear test, whose setup needs the banner; the 9 passing are the Fix 24 tests, the CMD-2 parent-refresh control, the 2 old dialog tests, the confirm-default control, and the AUD-1 empty-trail control — correct).
- **Fix 32b (CodeRabbit review 6031586408 on PR #163 head 83200bc, 2 Minors + 2 Nitpicks, all verified VALID):** (1) AuditLog superseded-request race — `fetchEntries` was recreated on every `isArabic` change and the mount effect re-ran it with no cancellation, so a late failure from the superseded request raised a banner over a successfully loaded trail and a late success overwrote entries and reset the page. Fix: a `requestSeq` generation guard — only the latest request writes `entries`/`page`/`loadError`/`loading` (the 401/auth routing is untouched: a dead session still logs out via the panel). (2) Resolve closed the wrong dialog — `handleResolveSos` did `setConfirmResolve(null)` unconditionally on success, so cancelling A's dialog, opening B's, and A's response landing dismissed B's dialog even though B was never resolved. Fix: functional update `setConfirmResolve((current) => (current?.id === sos.id ? null : current))`. Nitpick 1: removed the two `: any[]` annotations in the admin-panel logout assertions (house rule: no `any` in new types). Nitpick 2: the ADM-3 tests never exercised the pending state — +2 tests (button disabled while pending, second click issues no second POST, resolve → login surface; rejection re-enables the button + error toast + stays logged in).
- **Tests (32b, 5 new):** `tests/components/audit-log.test.tsx` +2 (late failure from a superseded request raises no banner; late success from a superseded request does not overwrite entries — both driven by a language-switch rerender). `tests/components/central-command.test.tsx` +1 (A's resolve landing while B's dialog is open leaves B's dialog open and B listed, removes A). `tests/components/admin-panel.test.tsx` +2 (pending logout, see above).
- **Fail-without-fix (32b, proven):** AuditLog + CentralCommand stash → the 2 race tests and the wrong-dialog test fail (3/3). The 2 pending-logout tests fail on pre-Fix-32 `AdminPanel.tsx` (checked out from main 5da2047, then restored byte-identical to 099da5a — `git diff 099da5a` empty).
- **Local gates (Fix 32):** lint 0, build pass, server 920/920 (85 files), React **1216/1216 (109 files) — clean full run, zero failures** (not even the known safe-evacuation flake this time). e2e not runnable in this VM (Firebase emulator needs Java) — CI covers it. Note: the first full React run was killed by a runtime restart mid-run (no result); this is the clean re-run.
- **Local gates (Fix 32 + 32b):** lint 0, build pass, server 920/920 (85 files), React **1221/1221 (109 files) — clean full run, zero failures** (1216 + 5 new 32b tests).
- **Fix 32 MERGED 2026-11-24 ~01:48 CET:** PR #163, merge 732f8d1 (parents 5da2047 + 250cefd). Tally now **91 merged / 188 remaining / 47 parts**.
- **Follow-up (not a blocker, D8/R9):** `AuditLog.fetchEntries` still rides a raw `fetch` with no timeout — a hung audit load hangs the spinner with no ceiling (same shape CMD-3 fixes for resolve). Switching to `apiFetch` is a separate change; record it for a later part.

## Fix 33 — Administration and command UI (BDG, STF, CLK, CMAP)
- **Branch:** `fix/wildfire-33-admin-command-ui` from main **732f8d1**. Prep: `~/workspace/wildfire-fix-prep/fix33-prep.md` (base 732f8d1; all citations re-verified by the parent at that commit). **All 9 decisions D1–D9 resolved to the prep's recommendations.** Findings are **LIVE, operator-facing defects** (BadgeManager/StaffManager inside AdminPanel, CommandLock gates the command surface, CommandMap is the command map). Triage title: "Administration and command UI (BDG, STF, CLK, CMAP)", triage row 64. One deliberate scope exception (D1): the single zod token in `server/routes/badges.ts` — the cap-clear has no client-only path, and Fix 41 must not re-edit that line.
- **Findings (all Minor):** **BDG-1** (`BadgeManager.tsx`) — clearing an existing expiry or usage cap sent `undefined`, JSON dropped it, the server retained the old limit while the UI reported success. **STF-1** (`StaffManager.tsx`) — `isSuper` conflated `admin` + `superadmin`, offering the superadmin-create option and delete/toggle on superadmin rows to a password-admin the server refuses (403); the row toggle button was not gated at all. **CLK-1** (`CommandLock.tsx`) — Enter bypassed the button-only busy guard; repeated Enters stacked verification requests and burned the 5-per-15-min login limiter. **CLK-2** (`CommandLock.tsx`) — the unlock rode a raw fetch with no deadline; a stalled link pinned `validating` (and the disabled button) forever. **CMAP-1** (`CommandMap.tsx`) — popup keys were positions, not entities: a moved user lost its popup at the next rebuild, and two reports at one location shared a key so the later marker's popup won.
- **Implemented:** BDG-1 — `handleSaveEdit` looks up the badge being edited and sends an explicit clear sentinel only when the badge had a value (hadValue pattern): expiry `""` (the server already nulls it), cap `null` (D1: schema `maxUses: z.number().int().positive().nullable().optional()` — the update line already writes `null` through, POST already null-tolerant, `capReached`/analytics already null-safe). STF-1 — split into `isTrueSuper` + `isStaffAdmin` + per-row `canModifyUser`/`canDeleteUser` mirroring the `server/routes/users.ts` matrix (true-super: anything; admin: any non-superadmin user + units; commander: own-unit agents only, toggle only, no delete); header label now distinguishes admin ("إدارة عامة — دون حسابات المشرف العام" / "Gestion globale — sauf comptes Super Admin") from true-super full access. CLK-1 — `if (validating) return` guard inside `handleUnlock` + `disabled={validating}` on the password input (same precedent as Fix 32 CMD-3's `resolvingId`). CLK-2 — unlock rides `apiFetch` (15 s ceiling; abort surfaces in the existing catch as a connection failure). CMAP-1 — keys are now bare stable IDs (`user:${deviceId}`, `report:${id}`); SOS/member keys already were.
- **Tests (16 new):** `tests/components/badge-manager.test.tsx` (4: expiry-clear sends `""` + untouched cap stays a number; cap-clear sends `null` + untouched expiry stays an ISO string; untouched empty fields stay omitted — control; create with empty optionals omits both keys — control). `tests/badges.test.ts` +2 (PUT `{maxUses: null}` clears the cap, `""` still 400s; PUT `{expiresAt: ""}` clears the expiry). `tests/components/staff-manager.test.tsx` (5: admin — no superadmin option, units kept, delete/toggle only on non-superadmin rows, admin label; superadmin — everything present — control; commander DZ16 — toggle only on own-unit agents, no delete anywhere, no superadmin option; agent — no create form, no toggle/delete; commander sees no units section). `tests/components/command-lock.test.tsx` (3: second Enter while pending issues no second POST; input disabled while pending; issued fetch carries an `AbortSignal`). `tests/components/command-map.test.tsx` (2: moved user keeps its popup; two co-located reports keep their own popups).
- **Test-design lesson (CLK-2):** the 15 s stall test was tried and dropped — vitest's fake timers do not fire Node's `AbortSignal.timeout`, so advancing 15 s never aborts the signal in jsdom. The ceiling is `apiFetch`'s, proven by the `AbortSignal` assertion; no test here can observe the abort without waiting a real 15 s. Recorded in the test file.
- **Fail-without-fix (per-file stash, all proven):** BadgeManager + badges.ts stash → 3 fail (both client sentinel tests + the server cap-null test; the server expiry-`""` test PASSES on old code — the expiry clear path already worked server-side, it is a control for the client sentinel, correct). StaffManager stash → 3/5 fail (admin, commander, agent; superadmin + units-commander controls pass — correct). CommandLock stash → 3/3 fail (incl. the double-Enter test — `fireEvent` reaches the handler on a disabled input, so the test is non-vacuous). CommandMap stash → 2/2 fail.
- **Local gates (Fix 33):** lint 0, build pass, server **922/922** (85 files; +2 D1 tests), React **1237/1237 (113 files) — clean full run, zero failures** (1221 + 14 new component tests + the 2 new server tests, which the React config also re-runs).
- **Fix 33b (CodeRabbit review 6032151179 on head 99186e6 — 1 Major + 1 Minor, both watch-verified and parent-verified in code):** (1) **Major** — `handleSaveEdit` read the clear decision from the live `badges` array at save time, but `loadAll` replaces it on every refresh while the edit form stays open: a limit added mid-edit made an unrelated save with an empty field send a clear for a limit the operator never touched. Fix: `editSnapshotRef` records `hadMaxUses`/`hadExpiresAt` in `startEdit`, and the clear decision reads only the snapshot. (2) **Minor** — a stored `null` cap is the normal uncapped state (POST writes `?? null`), but `startEdit` seeded it as the string `"null"` (field displayed `null`) and an untouched save sent `maxUses: null`. Fix: `typeof badge.maxUses === "number"` in both `startEdit` and the snapshot, and the `Badge` type declares `maxUses?: number | null`. **Tests +2** in `badge-manager.test.tsx` (6 total): the refresh-mid-edit snapshot test (proves the refresh lands via the re-issued GET count — the row stays in edit mode, so the display line never appears) and the null-cap display/omit test. **Fail-without-fix (33b, proven):** stashing `BadgeManager.tsx` fails exactly the 2 new tests; the 4 earlier tests pass on the 33 code.
- **Local gates (Fix 33 + 33b):** lint 0, build pass, server **922/922**, React **1238/1239** — the 1 failure is the known safe-evacuation Leaflet-init flake (passes 8/8 in isolation; the 16 Fix 33 tests pass 16/16 together). e2e not runnable in this VM (Firebase emulator needs Java) — CI covers it.

The 429 → "incorrect password" mislabel in CommandLock (D8) — the CLK-1 guard makes self-inflicted 429s nearly impossible; a shared-IP 429 is a different, server-wording-shaped defect. `AuditLog.fetchEntries` raw-fetch follow-up from Fix 32 still stands.

MAP-3 — condition flipped to `window.innerWidth < 640` + a `matchMedia("(max-width: 639px)")` change listener bumps a `narrowTick` state in the layer-control effect deps (fires only on breakpoint crossing; guarded with `typeof window.matchMedia === "function"` like the file's existing `ResizeObserver` guard — jsdom has neither). ## Fix 36 — Statistics, evacuation wording, proximity mute, geo mirror (STA, EVAC, PROX, GEO)
- **Branch:** `fix/wildfire-36-stats-evac-prox-geo` from main **fe74280**. Prep: `~/workspace/wildfire-fix-prep/fix36-prep.md` (base fe74280; all citations re-verified by the parent at that commit). **All 14 decisions D1–D14 resolved to the prep's recommendations.** Findings are **LIVE** (StatisticsPanel + SafeEvacuation mount via `layout/MainContent.tsx`; `useProximityAlerts` mounts once in `App.tsx:84`). Triage title: "Web radar, emergency display and mapping (RAD, MAP, STA)" — triage row 67. Client-only — no server change; Fix 33 D1's badges schema line untouched; `server/geo.ts` byte-identical.
- **Findings (all Minor):** **STA-2** (`StatisticsPanel.tsx:250`) — legend said "today at the far left" but the server sends buckets oldest→newest, so today is at the right. **STA-3** (`StatisticsPanel.tsx:51–65`) — non-OK mapped to null, rejections swallowed, zero bars with no loading/failure message: a dead `/api/history` looked exactly like a quiet month. **EVAC-3** (`SafeEvacuation.tsx:649/595/473`) — on OSRM failure the summary heading, the zone button ("active on the map" — no polyline is drawn) and the first spoken sentence still claimed a real road route, while the estimate disclosure existed only in small print; the voice case is worst (a listener never sees the disclosure). **PROX-1** (`useProximityAlerts.ts:428`) — the mute branch was a bare `return`, so a batch queued before the mute (pending entries + flush timer) survived and the tone rang on a muted device; sibling race: the siren checked mute before `await audioCtx.resume()` but never after. **GEO-1** (`src/utils/geo.ts` vs `server/geo.ts`) — the client narrowed Jijel/Skikda bounds and resolved by first match while the server keeps overlaps and resolves by smallest containing rectangle; a 0.05° grid scan found 4 disagreement classes (e.g. (36.8, 6.3) → Skikda client-side, Jijel server-side); the Algeria fallback also differed (`lat >= 19.0` vs server `lat > 18.0`).
- **Implemented:** STA-2 — legend corrected (ar "اليوم في أقصى اليمين", fr "Aujourd'hui à droite"); bar order untouched. STA-3 — `historyStatus` ("loading" | "ready" | "error") + `loadHistory` callback (AUD-1 precedent) with a loading line, an error line (`role="alert"`) and a retry button; bars render only when ready. EVAC-3 — heading, zone button and voice first step conditioned on `routeInfo?.source` (OSRM strings byte-identical; estimate: heading "مسار تقديري (خط مستقيم)" / "Itinéraire estimé (à vol d'oiseau)", button "التقدير محسوب" / "Estimation calculée", voice "تم حساب تقدير خط مستقيم — لا توجد طريق مرسومة على الخريطة." / "Une estimation à vol d'oiseau a été calculée — aucune route n'est tracée sur la carte."). Section heading, calculating overlay and empty-state left alone (they name the feature, not the outcome). PROX-1 — `isMutedRef` (live-mute readable inside async callbacks); the mute branch now clears `operatorPendingRef`, the flush timer and bumps the epoch WITHOUT touching announced memory (mute postpones, never swallows); `flushOperatorPending` and `beepOperatorTone` (after the resume await) re-check the ref; siren re-checks after `resume()` (D9). GEO-1 — client bounds restored to the server's (Skikda maxLng 7.4→7.5, Jijel 6.2→6.5), `find()` replaced by the server's smallest-area reduce (verbatim, strict `<` tie behaviour), Algeria fallback aligned to `lat > 18.0`; ARC-L12/ARC-M25 comments rewritten as a mirror claim.
- **Implementation lesson (PROX-1 — the mute branch MUST sit first):** the first implementation placed the clear inside the mute branch AFTER the `newOnes` computation — but `newOnes` is empty for already-queued reports (an already-pending report is not an escalation against itself), so the `newOnes.length === 0` early return skipped the clear entirely and the unmute test failed (pending batch dead with no timer, never re-announced). The branch now sits at the TOP of the effect, beside the trust-loss branch, before any newOnes computation. The flush guard alone had masked this in the mute test (the timer fired into the guard and died silently).
- **Tests (13 new):** `tests/components/statistics-panel-history.test.tsx` +4 (STA-2 ar/fr legend + first-bar-is-oldest proof; STA-3 error-with-retry-recovers; STA-3 loading line → bars). `tests/components/safe-evacuation.test.tsx` +3 (EVAC-3 heading/button/voice on the OSRM-500 path; `osrmFails` stub flag; the 6 existing OSRM-path assertions are the controls and keep passing). NEW `tests/components/proximity-alerts-mute.test.tsx` (3: queued tone silenced by muting; unmute re-announces the postponed batch; siren silent when muting during `resume()`; pending-vs-verified isolation trick so the tone counter measures one channel). `tests/client-threats.test.ts` +2 (GEO-1 probes (36.8, 6.3) → جيجل, (31.2, -8.5) → سوس ماسة). `tests/geo.test.ts` +1 (sampled-grid client≡server agreement, 0.5° step over lat 19–38 × lng −18–25 plus the two probes).
- **Fail-without-fix (per-file stash, all proven):** stashing all four source files fails exactly the 13 new tests (+1 pre-existing safe-evacuation Leaflet flake under parallel load, 11/11 in isolation). The unmute test fails on old code at the mute-step assertion (the queued tone rings while muted); the siren test fails on old code (siren rings after resume).
- **Local gates (Fix 36):** lint 0, build pass, server **925/925** (85 files; +3 from the `.test.ts` additions), React **1269/1269 (119 files)** — the full run showed 1268/1269 with the single failure being the known safe-evacuation Leaflet-init flake (failed once in the full run AND once in isolation under CPU pressure, then passed 11/11 on the second isolated re-run, per the recorded flake rule).
- **Fix 36b — CodeRabbit review 6033322713 on PR #167 (2 Minors + 1 Nitpick, all watch-verified and parent-verified in code):** (1) the STA-3 `historyCancelledRef` shared boolean was reset on every effect setup, so under StrictMode mount replay the first request was never cancelled and a late failure overwrote good chart data — replaced with a request-sequence guard (the AuditLog Fix 32 pattern): each `loadHistory` mints a sequence, results apply only while current, the effect cleanup invalidates, and the retry button supersedes in-flight loads the same way. (2) `beepOperatorTone` checked the flush epoch only in the commit helper AFTER playback — a batch muted and unmuted while `resume()` was pending still played its tone, then its commit was rejected and the report toned again (double tone) — the flush epoch is now passed into `beepOperatorTone` and checked after the mute re-check, before any oscillator is created; the retry path re-flushes with the current epoch and tones exactly once. (3) Nitpick: the grid test started at lat 19, so it could not catch the D11 fallback band regressing — explicit probes added: (18.5, 3) → Algérie - Autre, (36.6, 7.45) → Skikda, plus excluded-boundary controls (18, 3) and (37.5, 3) → out of coverage.
- **Fix 36b tests (+3):** StrictMode history race test (direct `<StrictMode>` JSX — the first request is parked, the replayed one resolves, rejecting the first must not show the error); epoch-during-resume test (mute + unmute while `resume()` pends → 0 starts on release, exactly 3 after the 1 s retry); geo boundary probes. **Fail-without-fix (36b, proven against the PR #167 branch head 99cae40):** exactly the 2 behavior tests fail on PR #167 code; the geo probes pass there (they guard the D11 change, which is in PR #167) and fail against pre-Fix-36 `geo.ts` (fe74280) together with the grid test.
- **Fix 36b test-authoring lesson (render-loop footgun):** the first version of the epoch test rendered the hook with an inline `reports` array literal — a new identity on every render. The siren effect calls `setActiveAlerts` unconditionally on every run, so each scan re-rendered with a new `reports` identity and re-ran the effect: a synchronous infinite render loop that hung vitest at 100% CPU with no output (1,069,278 scans in 30 s, diagnosed with a file-append trace). Tests 1–2 already used `initialProps`; test 3 only escaped because its siren scan parks inside `resume()`. Rule recorded in the test file header: reports go through `initialProps`, never an inline literal. (Not a hook bug — production passes stable state.)
- **Fix 36b gates:** lint 0; targeted run 72/72 (statistics-panel-history 9, proximity-alerts-mute 4, geo 30, safe-evacuation 11, client-threats 18); full React **1272/1272 (119 files) — clean full run, zero failures**.

Gates on the merged head: lint 0, build pass, server 925/925, React 1272/1272 clean; CodeRabbit clean after 36b; only instrumented (pre-existing emulator failure) + Vercel quota failed.

EVAC-3 residual: on estimate, no fire-proximity warning exists at all (no geometry to measure; the warning block is source-gated to `"osrm"`) — a straight-line fire check needs its own design. STA-3 residual: the `/api/history` fetch stays a raw `fetch` with no timeout — switching to `apiFetch` belongs to Fix 38 (API-1). ## Fix 37 — Web roster, team, SOS and session state (RSTR, TEAM, SOSM, SES)

- **Branch:** `fix/wildfire-37-roster-team-sos-session` from main **0f10737**. Prep: `~/workspace/wildfire-fix-prep/fix37-prep.md` (base 0f10737, all citations re-verified; dissection line numbers corrected inline). **All 8 decisions D1–D8 resolved to the prep's recommendations.** Client-only — no server change; `server/routes/roster.ts` (zod schema + `canonicalizePersonnel`) and `server/routes/users.ts` are the untouched backstop.
- **Scope correction (prep §6):** the brief said "SES-1 + RSTR-3", but triage row 68 assigns **five** findings — RSTR-3, RSTR-4, TEAM-2, SOSM-4, SES-1. All five are covered here; dropping any would need a triage change, not a silent omission.
- **Findings (all Minor, all LIVE except SES-1):**
  - **RSTR-3** (`RosterBoard.tsx`) — clearing a rank sent `""` through `updatePersonnel` verbatim; the server's `rank: z.string().min(1).optional()` 400s the whole save, and the failure path `await fetchRoster()` reloads the day, discarding every unsaved edit. Fix: an empty rank drops the `rank` key entirely (destructure-omit in `updatePersonnel`), so the PUT omits it and the server accepts it as absent. The input still shows `""` (`person.rank || ""`), so the UI is unchanged. `saveRoster` untouched (D1 — the failure reload itself is a shared-behavior design change, follow-up).
  - **RSTR-4** (`RosterBoard.tsx`) — `fetchStaff` filtered only `isActive`, so the assignment selector offered commanders and wrong-unit agents the server's `canonicalizePersonnel` rejects (same destructive reload on save). Fix: render-time filter (`assignableStaff` — active `agent` of the roster's unit) + an `addPersonnel` guard with the same predicate (defense in depth, mirroring the duplicate check). **Unit comparison mirrors the server's `toUnitId` exactly (Fix 37b):** `` `unit-${code.toLowerCase()}` `` applied to the user's raw code, compared directly with the canonical roster id — NO stripping (the first version stripped a leading `unit-` from both sides, which diverged from the server in both directions; see 37b below). Authority for the unit is `roster.unitId`, not the session's (null for a global admin). The `name` field is NOT guarded the same way (D2 — required, omitting doesn't fix it; pre-save validation is the follow-up).
  - **TEAM-2** (`TeamPanel.tsx`) — the browser heartbeat effect's cleanup cleared only the interval; a pending `getCurrentPosition` callback still sent a browser beat after native tracking took over, against the effect's own "only while no native FGS owns the stream" contract. Fix: a disposed flag **local to each effect run** (Fix 37b — the first version kept it in a shared ref that the next run's setup reset to `false`, so a GPS request from the previous run completing after a session/intervalMs re-run sent a beat with the previous session's token), closed over by that run's callbacks and set in that run's cleanup. **Critical detail:** the success path releases `beatBusyRef` BEFORE the early return, or the loop wedges forever (`tick()` returns early while busy). The disposed flag also covers effect re-runs for `session`/`intervalMs` (D5 — a `nativeActiveRef`-only check would not).
  - **SOSM-4** (`TrappedSOSModal.tsx`) — the profile-load effect overwrote `name`/`phone` unconditionally (only an unmount guard); a trapped person typing while the GET was pending lost their just-entered rescue contact details. Fix: per-field dirty refs (`nameTouchedRef`/`phoneTouchedRef`), one handler per field shared by both identity forms, checked in the effect. An "only fill when empty" guard would be **vacuous** here — the `[]`-deps effect closes over mount-time `""` forever (same lesson class as Fix 31b's `currentUtteranceRef` and Fix 36's `isMutedRef`).
  - **SES-1** (`src/lib/session.ts`) — **ALREADY FIXED by Fix 29b** (the `subscribeSessionPoll` else-branch calls `listener()` at once for late poll listeners; `subscribeStaffSession` gives the immediate snapshot + first-subscriber fetch). **No `session.ts` source change in Fix 37** — test-only: one regression test pinning the late *poll* listener arm (the late *staff* subscriber was already pinned in `tests/staff-session-shared.test.tsx`). A stale snapshot up to 60 s old for a late staff subscriber is the designed freshness bound, not a defect (considered and rejected in prep §1.5).
- **Tests (10 new, no new files):** roster-board +4 (RSTR-3a save-omits-rank + RSTR-3b present-rank control; RSTR-4a selector offers only the same-unit agent + RSTR-4b add-and-save control — the PUT stub simulates the server zod rule: present-but-empty rank 400s, omitted rank saves, and echoes the saved posts back); team-panel +2 (TEAM-2a parked GPS fix sends nothing after the native flip + control releasing without flipping still beats — deferred geolocation stub parks the callback; the Fix 36b render-loop lesson applies: the parked callback is released once, explicitly); trapped-sos-modal +3 (SOSM-4a typed name+phone survive + SOSM-4b per-field partial + control both fill — deferred profile response); staff-session +1 (SES-1 late poll listener probes at once — placed after the visibility test, before the staleness test, per the file's ordering note).
- **Fail-without-fix (proven):** stashing the three source files fails exactly the 5 non-control new tests (RSTR-3a, RSTR-4a, TEAM-2a, SOSM-4a, SOSM-4b) — all controls pass. Deleting the `subscribeSessionPoll` else-branch fails exactly the SES-1 test (waitFor timeout); `session.ts` restored byte-identical afterwards (no diff).
- **Local gates (Fix 37):** lint 0, build pass, server **926/926** (85 files; 925 + the 36b geo boundary test), React **1282/1282 (119 files) — clean full run, zero failures**.
- **Fix 37b — CodeRabbit review 6034317206 on PR #168 (1 Major + 1 Minor, both watch-verified and parent-verified in the server code at 0f10737):** (1) **Major** — the Fix 37 `normalizeUnitId` stripped a leading `unit-` from both sides, but the server's `toUnitId` (`server/routes/units.ts:11`) is `` `unit-${code.toLowerCase()}` `` with NO stripping, and `canonicalizePersonnel` compares `toUnitId(staff.unitId) !== unitId`. Divergence in both directions: a raw `UNIT-DZ16` code is accepted server-side (`unit-unit-dz16`) but was hidden client-side, and a raw `unit-dz16` code is rejected server-side but was offered. Fix: `toCanonicalUnitId` mirrors the server exactly and compares directly with the canonical roster id. (2) **Minor** — the Fix 37 disposed flag lived in a shared ref that the next run's setup reset to `false`; a GPS request from the previous run completing after a session/intervalMs re-run sent a beat with the previous session's token (the `sessionRef` check runs after the await, so it prevented the state commit but not the send). ## Fix 38 — API helpers and team verdicts (API-1, API-2, TEAM-7, TEAM-8, all Minor)

- **Branch:** `fix/wildfire-38-api-team-verdicts` from main `07adba8`. Prep: `~/workspace/wildfire-fix-prep/fix38-prep.md` (122 lines, base 07adba8, all citations re-verified with `git show`). **SCOPE CORRECTION (prep): the brief said "API-1/2 + TEAM-7" but triage row 69 assigns FOUR findings — API-1, API-2, TEAM-7, TEAM-8 — plus the STA-3 residual (the `/api/history` raw-fetch switch, assigned to Fix 38 under API-1).** Client-only — no server change; `server/routes/teams.ts` schemas are the mirrored authority. **All 8 decisions D1–D8 resolved to the prep's recommendations.** **Prep correction (parent): the prep claimed the join UI renders `join-failed` as "تعذر الانضمام — تحقق من الرمز." — WRONG: `TeamPanel.tsx:401–404` renders `result.message || <fallback>`, and the message IS "join-failed", so the literal code is what appears. The TEAM-8 TeamPanel test asserts the literal text.**
- **Findings (all Minor, all LIVE except API-2 which is dormant):** **API-1** (`src/utils/api.ts:20–27`) — the 5xx branch of `fetchWithRetry` never recorded anything in `lastError` (assigned only in the `catch`), so an exhausted all-503 run threw the bare "Failed after N retries" and a mixed run reported the OLD transport error, contradicting the ARC-L14 comment promising the last underlying error. **API-2** (`src/utils/api.ts:69`) — `cacheGet`'s `ttlMs` parameter is never referenced (only the stored absolute expiry is checked); zero consumers repo-wide (verified by grep; the only other `cacheGet` is an unrelated server function in `server/routes/geo.ts:46`). **TEAM-7** (`src/utils/teamSession.ts:294–300`) — finite but out-of-schema optional GPS metadata (accuracy >10,000, heading outside 0–360, speed outside 0–80) was sent unfiltered; the server 400s the entire beat (`teams.ts:490–494`), so a coarse fix kept a member off the command map while the panel reported only a transient blip. **TEAM-8** (`src/utils/teamSession.ts`) — on a 2xx with a null/unparseable body, all four helpers threw `TypeError` instead of returning their verdict (join :230, probe :274–278, heartbeat :316–317, flip :366); in TeamPanel this stranded `joining` (button `disabled={joining}`, :627), `flipBusy` (:719), and `resuming` (spinner :558, starts true on a persisted session).
- **Implemented:** API-1 — `lastError = new Error(\`HTTP ${res.status}\`)` in the HTTP-failure branch before the retry-delay check (last failure wins in every order; retry schedule, 4xx-immediate contract, abort behavior untouched). API-2 — the dead `ttlMs` parameter removed from `cacheGet` (`cacheSet`'s live `ttlMs` untouched). TEAM-7 — shared `inSchemaRange()` sanitizer (accuracy [0, 10_000], heading [0, 360], speed [0, 80]) used by `sendTeamHeartbeat` for all three fields and by `flipMissionOnScene` for accuracy; **omit, not clamp** (clamping fabricates precision); TeamPanel untouched. TEAM-8 — one null-guard per helper after the `!res.ok` early return, returning the existing failure vocabulary: join → `{ ok: false, message: "join-failed" }`; probe → the transport-style non-verdict `{ ok: false, fatal: undefined, heartbeatIntervalMs: 15_000 }`; heartbeat → `{ ok: false, transient: true }`; flip → `{ ok: false, code: "transient" }`; **no TeamPanel change** (after the guards the helpers are provably non-throwing). STA-3 residual — `StatisticsPanel.loadHistory`'s raw `fetch("/api/history?days=30")` → `apiFetch("/api/history?days=30", "GET")` (15 s ceiling); the Fix 36b sequence guard wraps the promise chain, not the fetch call, so it survives untouched.
- **Tests (+14):** fetch-retry +2 (all-503 names the status; mixed failures report the LAST error); NEW `tests/cache.test.ts` +3 (round-trip; expired → null + key removed; missing/corrupt → null — jsdom pragma is load-bearing, `cacheSet` throws in node); team-session +7 (TEAM-7 omit-all-three, boundary control at the exact inclusive maxima, flip omits accuracy; TEAM-8 one per helper + the unparseable variant on join); statistics-panel-history +1 (the issued history fetch carries an `AbortSignal` — Fix 33 precedent); team-panel +1 (null-bodied join re-enables the button). **Fail-without-fix (proven):** stashing the three source files fails exactly the 10 new non-control tests (API-1a/b, TEAM-7 omit + flip, TEAM-8 ×4, history ceiling, team-panel join); the cache tests pass on old code by design (the defect is the dead parameter, proven by grep + lint, not by a test); the TEAM-7 boundary test passes on old code (control). **One parallel-run flake during the proof:** the pre-existing "flips en_route → on_scene" team-panel test failed once in the 5-file parallel stash run and passed in isolation on the same old code — the known timing-flake class, not a discrimination.

- **Branch:** `fix/wildfire-39-report-registration-inputs` from main `de84d1d`. Prep: `~/workspace/wildfire-fix-prep/fix39-prep.md` (119 lines, base 07adba8, all citations re-verified with `git show` — no files modified by the prep agent). **SCOPE CORRECTION (prep): the brief said "VOL-2 + RSUB-1/2" but triage row 70 assigns FIVE findings — FRM-1, VOL-2, UPL-1, RSUB-1, RSUB-2 — plus the Fix 29 country-hint follow-up, explicitly assigned to Fix 39.** Client-only — no server change; `server/routes/volunteers.ts:24` (phone regex), `server/routes/reports.ts:328–336` (multipart image gate), `:315–323` (durable replay by `clientGeneratedId`) are the mirrored authority. **All 8 decisions D1–D8 resolved to the prep's recommendations (D8: remove the duplicated `setErrorMsg("")` at `VolunteerRegistration.tsx:49/:52` — it sits inside the edited lines).** **Prep line-number correction (parent): the prep's maghrebRegions section ranges were off by one (comment lines counted) — the real entry ranges are DZ :22–79, TN :82–105, MA :108–119, LY :122–143 (116 entries; verified by section-comment grep).**
- **Findings (all Minor, all LIVE):** **FRM-1** (`ReportForm.tsx`) — seven label/control pairs with a bare `<label>` (no `htmlFor`) and a control with no `id` (latitude, longitude, wilaya, location name, description, badge code, reporter name/phone); clicking a label focused nothing and screen readers got no programmatic name. The badge field is hidden by default (`reporterType` defaults to `"citizen"`). **VOL-2** (`VolunteerRegistration.tsx:50–54`) — the registration POST rode a raw `fetch` with no timeout; `isSubmitting` cleared only in `finally`, so a stalled request left the form disabled forever. **UPL-1** (`observatoryUpload.ts`) — an empty data URL yields a zero-byte Blob (truthy) on BOTH decode paths, appended with `imageDropped: false`, contradicting the helper's own contract and tripping the server's multipart image gate. **RSUB-1** (`useReportSubmission.ts:60–62`) — the 15 s timer was cleared after fetch resolved to headers; both `res.json()` calls (:78, :93) were outside the deadline, so a stalled body hung the submit spinner. **RSUB-2** (`useReportSubmission.ts:93`) — the success-body `res.json()` had no catch (unlike the error branch :77–81); a truncated body threw `SyntaxError` before validation/reconciliation and the caller showed a failure for an accepted report. **Fix 29 follow-up** — MA/LY `05/06/07` national numbers normalize DZ-first; the volunteer form already collects the wilaya (required field), so the hint is free.
- **Implemented:** FRM-1 — one `htmlFor` + matching `id` per pair (`report-lat`, `report-lng`, `report-wilaya`, `report-location-name`, `report-description`, `report-badge-code`, `report-reporter-name`, `report-reporter-phone` — static ids, single mount at `MainContent.tsx:169`). VOL-2 — the POST rides `apiFetch` (15 s house ceiling; drop-in: same Content-Type header, same JSON body shape) + the duplicated `setErrorMsg("")` removed. Country hint — `country: "DZ" | "TN" | "MA" | "LY"` added to `MaghrebRegion` and populated on all 116 entries per section; `normalizeMaghrebPhone(value, countryHint?)` resolves ONLY the `0[567]` collision by hint (MA → +212, LY → +218, DZ/TN/none → today's DZ-first); prefixed numbers pass through before the table, the TN 8-digit shape is untouched, and the no-hint default is byte-identical; `VolunteerRegistration` derives the hint from the selected wilaya string (`MAGHREB_REGIONS.find((w) => \`${w.nameAr} (${w.nameFr})\` === wilaya)?.country`). UPL-1 — one check after both decoders, before the append: `if (blob && blob.size === 0) blob = null;` (+ warn). RSUB-1 — the SAME 15 s timer stays armed through body consumption (cleared in a `finally` after all body work); a body-phase abort is routed by status: error status → the existing error construction with `serverMsg` undefined; 2xx → the accepted-but-syncing path (RSUB-2's path). RSUB-2 — the :93 parse is wrapped; on failure `newReport = null` and the existing malformed-object path runs (`fetchData()` + `SubmissionAccepted`, no `admitServerReport`, no mesh broadcast) — no new return site.
- **Implementation notes (parent):** (1) The RSUB restructure moved the transport catch INSIDE the timer's try (fetch-phase catch fans out + rethrows; the outer `finally` clears the timer on every path). (2) `newReport` is typed `any`, not `unknown` — the original `const newReport = await res.json()` was implicitly `any`, and narrowing an `unknown` through the aliased `reportIsValid` flag does not survive the try/catch assignment (tsc TS2345/TS2698); the `isValidReport` gate below is the check that matters. (3) The first FRM-1 edit pass inserted `id=` mid-attribute (naive `>` search hit the `=>` in `onChange`) and the second pass put it after the self-closing `/` — both caught by lint; the file was restored from HEAD and re-done with a brace/quote-aware tag-end scanner. (4) The observatory-hooks test appends twice duplicated the enclosing describe's closing `});` (the anchor already contained it) — caught by the transform parse error, removed. (5) jsdom does NOT move focus on a label click (proven by a minimal probe: plain label+input, focus stays on body), so the FRM-1 click test asserts the label's `.control` property instead — the association the browser's click-to-focus runs on.
- **Tests (+12):** phone +4 (MA hint → +212, LY hint → +218, explicit DZ hint = default, prefixed passthrough ignores the hint — control); volunteer-registration +2 (the issued registration fetch carries an `AbortSignal` — Fix 33 precedent, fake timers do NOT fire `AbortSignal.timeout`; Morocco wilaya + bare 06 → POST body `+212612345678`); NEW `tests/components/report-form-labels.test.tsx` +2 (all seven fields labelled via `getByLabelText`, badge field after clicking the "متطوع معتمد" role button; each label's `.control` is its field — jsdom limitation noted above; mount needs the `offlineDraftStore`/`badgeStore` mocks from `report-form-hooks.test.tsx` + a fetch safety-net stub); observatory-hooks +4 (UPL-1 fetch path + atob-fallback path — empty data URL → `imageDropped: true`, no image part; RSUB-1 stalled 2xx body — signal-wired deferred `json()` + fake timers past 15 s → `SubmissionAccepted`; RSUB-2 throwing `json()` → `SubmissionAccepted`, no mesh broadcast). **Fail-without-fix (proven):** stashing the six source files fails exactly the 10 new non-control tests (labels ×2, volunteer ×2, phone hint MA/LY, UPL-1 ×2, RSUB-1 by 5 s test timeout, RSUB-2); the DZ-hint and prefixed-passthrough tests pass on old code (controls). **Proof artifact:** the first stash run also failed 4 pre-existing "report confirmation" tests — NOT a discrimination: the RSUB-1 timeout killed its test mid-flight and leaked fake timers (`finally { vi.useRealTimers(); }` never ran); a re-run excluding RSUB-1 failed exactly UPL-1 ×2 + RSUB-2 and the 4 confirmation tests passed.

- **Fix 40b — CodeRabbit Minor (review 6036731667, "Read current drafts when a sync starts", verified VALID in code):** `syncOfflineDrafts` snapshotted the closed-over `offlineDrafts`; a failed sync keeps the draft (no length change), so the next `online` event re-synced the same snapshot and `retryCount` never advanced past the first failure (mount 1→2, event stayed 2). Fix: `offlineDraftsRef` mirrors the list (render-time assignment, the `isMutedRef` precedent in `useProximityAlerts.ts`); `syncOfflineDrafts` reads `offlineDraftsRef.current` at start; trigger deps unchanged (no automatic re-retries). Test +1 in `tests/report-form-hooks.test.tsx` (mount failure then event failure: retryCount 1→2→3, `onSubmit` ×2). Fail-without-fix proven: stashing the source fails exactly the new test. Local commit eaf140a; pushed via browser as 3 commits (46d42ef FIX_PLAN, 7ba3a08 hook, 5c39453 tests — GitHub's web upload cannot put files from different directories in one commit, so the planned 2-commit push split into 3; the final tree is byte-identical to eaf140a, tree 62662a3d); head **5c39453**. This closes the Fix 40 stale-snapshot follow-up (the DRAFT-1 stale-snapshot line is now resolved, not a follow-up). **Follow-up (CodeRabbit Minor, review 6037334678, "Heavy lift", no patch — recorded per the Fix 31c precedent, not blocking):** the Fix 28b restore (`setOfflineDrafts(draftSnapshot)` in the catch) after a successful `replaceOfflineDrafts` + failed `removeOfflineDrafts` changes length 0→1, and the Fix 40 mount-sync effect re-runs and re-submits the already-accepted draft. A real loop needs a persistent split IndexedDB failure (replace committing while remove fails on every iteration; in practice both fail together, in which case length never changes and no loop occurs); impact is bounded by `clientGeneratedId` idempotency (no duplicate server records, no data loss). A proper fix means redesigning the Fix 28b persistence-error contract (track accepted IDs across a failed removal or bound automatic retries) — a design decision for a later part.
- **Follow-ups (not blockers):** CodeRabbit Low (PR #170 summary, advisory, no actionable comment): (1) the new registration timeout can report a connection error after the server has committed the application — a retry is dedup-protected but the duplicate response becomes a generic failure rather than confirming receipt; (2) on the stalled-success recovery path, the POST deadline does not bound the wait for the user-visible acknowledgment — after the body read times out, submission waits for the multi-dataset refresh (`fetchData()`) before releasing the form or showing "accepted — syncing". VOL-2 residual — manual cancel button (the 15 s ceiling is the recovery deadline the finding asks for; a visible cancel is a UX addition for a later part). FRM-1 adjacent — severity group has no `fieldset`/`legend`; the hidden file input has no accessible name (the visible attach button is the accessible control). RSUB-1 adjacent — `useReportConfirmation`'s outer budget spans enrollment; whether each retry deserves a fresh budget is a separate decision. ## Fix 41 — Server routes and request handling (SRV, ADM, AUD, CMD, NTF, all Minor)

- **Branch:** `fix/wildfire-41-server-routes-handling` from main `f8975ad`. Prep: `~/workspace/wildfire-fix-prep/fix41-prep.md` (base 0725ee3, all citations re-verified with `git show` at f8975ad — Fix 40 touched only web files, so every server citation holds). **SCOPE CORRECTION (prep): the brief said "ADM-5 + AUD-3" but triage row 72 assigns FIVE findings — SRV-2, ADM-5, AUD-3, CMD-6, NTF-2.** Server-only — no client change. **All 7 decisions D1–D7 resolved to the prep's recommendations.**
- **Findings (all Minor):** **SRV-2** (`server/server.ts:197`) — `app.listen` has no `error` listener (only `upgrade` at :212); `startServer().catch` at :225 can never see an async `EADDRINUSE` — the process crashes with an unhandled `error` event instead of the promised actionable log + exit 1. **ADM-5** (`server/routes/admin.ts:131–140`) — admin logout discards the `revokeAdminSession` boolean and always returns success; the durable-write-failed + restart hole (the in-process `revocationSeen` map already protects this process). The fix already exists one route over: staff logout (`server/routes/auth.ts:100–103`) returns 503 `"Session revocation could not be persisted"`. **AUD-3** (`server/routes/audit.ts:110–117`) — GET `/api/audit` returns the DB list whenever nonempty, hiding recent entries that only exist in `memoryAudit`/`pendingAudit`. **CMD-6** (`server/routes/command.ts:114–123`) — badge ceiling: a retry of a just-rejected 6th code finds `seenAt` and skips the size check entirely, so the retry gets a normal 200 beat. **NTF-2 (ALREADY FIXED — test-only)** — the dissection's early return is gone; Fix 8c/8e already added the verified-preferences repair path with token proof in both SDK branches (`server/routes/notifications.ts:370–388` admin SDK, `:428–445` client SDK), and the existing `tests/notifications-subscribe-update.test.ts` 8c/8e describes assert the exact NTF-2 scenario. No source change, no new tests — run the existing describes, confirm green, record NTF-2 as "closed by Fix 8c/8e, verified in Fix 41".
- **Implemented:** SRV-2 — NEW module `server/listen.ts` exporting `waitForListening(server)`: resolves once bound, rejects on an async bind error, removes the other listener on settle, `server.listening` fast path; `server.ts` imports it and awaits `waitForListening(httpServer)` after the `app.listen` call (the one-line wiring is verified by code reading only — `server.ts` self-starts on import, so it cannot be imported in tests; SRV-1 precedent). ADM-5 — admin logout mirrors staff logout verbatim (same 503 message; `const revoked = token ? await revokeAdminSession(token, "logout") : true;` — no-token → 200, accepted per D2). AUD-3 — GET merges DB + `memoryAudit`, dedupes by `id` (DB-first on collision), sorts timestamp desc, caps 100; response shape unchanged; a DB read failure still falls back to memory only (`entries ?? []`). CMD-6 — restructure: insert-if-unseen, prune always, check always (NOT check-then-insert). NTF-2 — no source change.
- **Tests (+9, all server):** ADM-5 +3 — NEW `tests/admin-logout-revocation.test.ts` (mounts the real `adminRouter`; harness follows `tests/admin-revocation.test.ts`: `vi.mock` firebase + fs with controllable `docSet`, stubs for `../server/db.js`, `../server/live.js`, `../server/email.js`, `../server/routes/notifications.js`; token via `Authorization: Bearer generateAdminToken("admin")` — no cookie-parser needed; finding test: `docSet` → false → POST `/api/admin/logout` → 503 + exact staff message; control 1: `docSet` → true → 200; control 2: no token → 200 + `docSet` never called). AUD-3 +3 — extended `tests/audit-durability.test.ts` (finding test: `collectionGet` returns 2 hour-old durable rows + `logAdminAction` with `docSet` failing → GET returns all 3, newest-first; dedupe test: same id in both lists → appears exactly once — does NOT discriminate, guards the merge; cap test: 100 durable + 5 newer memory entries → exactly 100, newest-first, memory ones included). CMD-6 +1 — extended `tests/heartbeat.test.ts` (ONE test, four phases — the module-level `heartbeatBadgesPerIp` map is shared across the file; fresh `uniqueDevice()` per attempt for the 3 s per-device throttle; fake codes `CMD6-1`…`CMD6-6`, unknown codes fall through to a 200 citizen beat, 30/min limiter allows 8 requests: (a) codes 1–5 → 200; (b) retry code 1 → 200; (c) code 6 → 429 `"Too many badge codes from this address."`; (d) retry code 6 → **429**). SRV-2 +2 — NEW `tests/server-listen.test.ts` (bind server A to port 0, read its port, `waitForListening(B.listen(port))` → rejects with `code === "EADDRINUSE"`; control: free ephemeral port → resolves; `if (server.listening)` guard before `close()` in `finally` — `close()` on a never-listening server throws). NTF-2 +0 — the existing 10-test `notifications-subscribe-update.test.ts` suite ran green as part of the targeted run.

All 13 other targeted tests pass, including the dedupe test and every control. **Implementation incident (parent-caught, lesson (hh) again):** the heartbeat test append duplicated the describe's closing `});` (the matched tail already contained it) — caught by the transform parse error; the stray close before the new test was removed and brace depth verified 0 with a node counter before re-running.

React **1323/1324 (123 files)** — the single failure is the known safe-evacuation Leaflet flake ("a failed zone refresh keeps the existing list and the active route", 5,095 ms timeout in the full run; passes in 326 ms in isolation; Fix 41 touches zero client files). test:e2e not runnable in this VM (Firebase emulator needs Java) — CI covers it.
- **Fix 41 follow-ups (not blockers):** ADM-5 adjacent — distinguish "nothing to revoke" from "write failed" (a tri-state would touch the shared function + the staff caller + tests; the D2 wrinkle — an invalid/expired token also 503s — is accepted). SRV-2 adjacent — none (post-resolution runtime errors escaping the catch is normal; recorded so a reviewer does not re-raise it). AUD-3 adjacent — the merged list is capped at 100 while `memoryAudit` holds 200; no pagination on GET `/api/audit`. CMD-6 adjacent — the ceiling is per-IP and supertest shares one IP (a `resetHeartbeatBadgesPerIp` test export would help but adds a test-only export to production code).
- **Fix 41b — CodeRabbit review 6037710493 on PR #172 (2 Minors + 1 Nitpick, all watch-verified and parent-verified in code — both Minors are regressions Fix 41 itself introduced):** (1) **Minor (inline 4678757788, `admin.ts:139`)** — the Fix 41 logout cleared the cookie BEFORE the 503 branch. On a failed revocation write the client got 503 with its cookie already gone; the token is httpOnly, the client holds no other copy, so a retry sent no token → `revoked = true` → 200 without ever re-attempting the write. The durable revocation was never persisted, yet the client received success — and after a restart the still-valid token worked again for up to 24 h. Pre-Fix-41 the failure was silently swallowed with 200; Fix 41 made it visible (503) but destroyed the retry path. Fix: clear the cookie ONLY after the revocation persisted (the retry re-presents the token; `revokeAdminSession`'s in-process set is idempotent and the write is re-tried). (2) **Minor (inline 4678757813, `command.ts:122`)** — the Fix 41 restructure inserted the 6th code BEFORE the ceiling check, so a rejected code stayed in the per-IP map at size 6 and every later retry of an ACCEPTED code 429d until the window expired (pre-Fix-41 the check sat inside `if (!seenAt)`, so a seen code's retry skipped it). Fix: prune first, then reject an unseen code that would exceed the ceiling WITHOUT inserting it — the ceiling now counts only admitted codes. (3) **Nitpick (review body, `audit.ts:124`)** — the merged-list map was `Map<string, any>`; typed as `AuditEntry` (the `logAdminAction` entry shape, declared in the handler) — the pre-existing `entries: any[] | null` and `memoryAudit: any[]` are untouched.
- **Fix 41b tests (+1 new test; the CMD-6 case is an added phase inside the existing test):** admin-logout +1 (failed logout → 503 + NO `Set-Cookie` header; retry with the same token after `docSet` recovers → 200 + `Set-Cookie` clears the token); heartbeat CMD-6 + phase (e) (retry of accepted code 1 after the 6th-code rejection → 200). **Fail-without-fix (41b, proven against the Fix 41 commit 9802b2a):** stashing the three source files fails exactly the 2 new 41b checks; all 16 other targeted tests pass (the audit `any` change is a type change — no behavior test, as expected).
- **Fix 41b gates:** lint 0, build pass, server **952/952** (88 files; 951 + 1). React **1322/1325 (123 files)** — the 3 failures are all known flakes, proven by re-run: the report-form-hooks "loads persisted drafts" test and the team-panel "flips en_route → on_scene" test both pass in the targeted 3-file re-run; the safe-evacuation "deactivated centre" failure is the Leaflet-init flake class (the re-run flaked a DIFFERENT safe-evacuation test at 5,113 ms, then passed clean — the documented "flakes once in isolation under CPU pressure, re-run again" pattern). Fix 41b touches zero client files. test:e2e not runnable in this VM (Firebase emulator needs Java) — CI covers it.
- **Fix 41c — CodeRabbit review 6037976723 on PR #172 (1 new Minor, watch-verified and parent-verified in code — the D2 follow-up, now necessary):** the Fix 41 503 × Fix 41b cookie-retention interaction turned a legacy jti-less (or invalid/expired) token's logout into a permanent dead-end. `revokeAdminSession` returned a boolean that conflated "nothing to revoke" with "the write failed" (`middleware.ts:55–75`, docstring included), so the admin route 503d with the cookie retained — and every retry 503d again, forever, because the token can never produce a durable entry. Pre-Fix-41 the same logout returned 200 and cleared the cookie. Watch 2 confirmed the two 41b Minors + the Nitpick are SATISFIED in the new head's code (cookie clear after the revoked check, prune-first + reject-without-insert, `AuditEntry` typing), and all own checks were green except the two known non-blockers. **Fix 41c implements the D2 tri-state:** `revokeAdminSession` now returns `"revoked" | "nothing-to-revoke" | "failed"` — verify failures and jti-less tokens return `"nothing-to-revoke"` (the write is never attempted), a present jti with a failed/thrown `docSet` returns `"failed"`, a persisted write returns `"revoked"`. Both callers use it: admin logout 503s + keeps the cookie ONLY on `"failed"` (legacy/invalid tokens log out cleanly, 200 + cookie cleared), and staff logout (`auth.ts`) gets the same tri-state AND the same cookie order — its old code cleared the cookie BEFORE the 503, the exact 41b bug class on the staff side. Staff tokens always carry a jti (`generateStaffToken` adds one), so no legacy staff-token class exists. `AdminPanel.tsx:132`'s stale "the route discards the boolean" boundary comment is corrected to the tri-state truth (comment-only).
- **Fix 41c tests (+1 new test; 3 assertions updated to the tri-state):** admin-logout +1 (legacy jti-less token → 200 + `Set-Cookie` clears + `docSet` never called); `admin-revocation.test.ts` persists-assertion `true` → `"revoked"`, legacy-assertion `false` → `"nothing-to-revoke"`; `staff-sessions.test.ts` revocation-assertion `true` → `"revoked"`. **Fail-without-fix (41c, proven against head 8068484):** stashing the three source files fails exactly the 4 expected checks — the 3 updated tri-state assertions (they track the new return type) + the new legacy-logout finding test (old code 503s it); all 23 other targeted tests pass, including the 41b tests and the staff 503 test.

React **1324/1326 (123 files)** — the 2 failures are both known flakes, proven by re-run: the dataset-outages "returns 503 past the absolute age bound" test passes in the targeted 2-file re-run (the documented dataset-outages:114 flake, hit many times), and the safe-evacuation "deactivated centre" failure is the Leaflet-init flake class (the re-run flaked a DIFFERENT safe-evacuation test at 5,049 ms, then passed 11/11 alone). Fix 41c touches zero client behavior files (AdminPanel change is a comment only). test:e2e not runnable in this VM (Firebase emulator needs Java) — CI covers it.

## Fix 43 — Server routes and request handling (SOS, TEAM, USR, VOL, all Minor)

- **Branch:** `fix/wildfire-43-sos-team-usr-vol` from main `c18f9cd`. Prep: `~/workspace/wildfire-fix-prep/fix43-prep.md` (142 lines, base 197e0bb; all load-bearing citations re-verified by the parent with `git show` at c18f9cd — Fix 42's diff touches only reports/roster/satellite/tests/FIX_PLAN, zero overlap with Fix 43 files). **NO scope correction — triage row 74 assigns FIVE findings. NOT server-only (see SOS-5):** the triage row's "Server routes" label is incomplete — SOS-5's fix requires a client validator + type + render-guard change, or the client's SOS polling freezes. **All 7 decisions D1–D7 resolved to the prep's recommendations.**
- **Findings (all Minor, all LIVE):** **SOS-5** (`server/routes/sos.ts:232–233`) — a no-fix SOS becomes fabricated 0,0 on the public feed: `anonymizeSos` does `Math.round(null * 100)/100 === 0`; nulls persist correctly at :452–453; only public GET `/` (:257) uses it — `/full` already retains nulls. **SOS-6** (`server/routes/sos.ts:612`, `:614`; `server/fs.ts:359–470`) — idempotent re-dispatch duplicates only the local fallback record: `appendSosDispatch` returns `"ok"` with zero writes on re-dispatch (ARC-R4), and the route cannot tell re-dispatch from fresh dispatch, so it pushes a second entry into `memorySos` and returns a fresh en-route item. **TEAM-6** (`server/routes/teams.ts:285–300`) — fix age dropped from the command roster response: heartbeat stores `fixTimeMs` (`server/teamRegistry.ts:103`, `listPositions` returns it) but the GET `/` mapper omits it; the schema comment promises surfaces render it, yet no client code reads the field at all. **USR-2** (`server/routes/users.ts:158–165`) — roster cleanup claims completion while every write failed: `Promise.all` discards `docSet` booleans (`false` on failure), then logs "Removed deleted agent…" whenever `affected.length > 0`; the response `{success: true}` does not claim cleanup — only the log lies. **VOL-3** (`server/routes/volunteers.ts:102`) — a successful empty DB refresh preserves deleted registrations: `loadRegs` refreshes only when `fromDb !== null && fromDb.length > 0` — a successful `[]` read keeps serving stale `memoryRegs` indefinitely, contradicting its own L2 comment.
- **Implemented:** SOS-5 — `anonymizeSos` preserves nulls (`sos.lat == null ? null : Math.round(...)`, same for lng); `isValidSosItem` accepts null coords exactly when `hasLocation === false` (a located SOS with null coords is still malformed — else the SOS poll freezes, §1.1 trap); `TrappedSOS.lat/lng` widened to `number | null` + `hasLocation?: boolean`; CommandMap skips the marker for a no-fix SOS (type-predicate filter, so `L.marker` still receives `number`); `handleFocusSos` no-ops on null coords (the SOS still appears in SosPanel). SOS-6 — `SosDispatchResult` widened with `"already_dispatched"` (both ARC-R4 return sites in fs.ts, zero writes preserved); the route returns the existing dispatch from the memory record (freshly built item + flag when the process restarted and `memorySos` lacks the SOS) with `alreadyDispatched: true`, 200 — and skips the memory push. TEAM-6 — `fixTimeMs: position?.fixTimeMs ?? null` in the GET mapper (never fabricated — absent on legacy clients stays null). USR-2 — the sweep captures the `Promise.all` results; the success log runs only when every write succeeded, otherwise a `logger.warn` names the failed day count; the DELETE response surfaces additive `rosterCleanup: { days, removed, failed }` (Fix 41 `missionsCleared` precedent); the deletion itself stays 200 — a partial cleanup is a warning, not a deletion failure. VOL-3 — `if (fromDb !== null)` — refresh on any successful read including `[]`; `null` (read failure) keeps the stale memory fallback.
- **Tests (+12 new; 2 assertions updated):** SOS-5 +1 server (`tests/sos.test.ts` — POST a no-fix SOS, then GET `/api/sos` → `lat: null, lng: null, hasLocation: false`; located control on the same feed still rounded 36.75/7.6) +1 validator (`tests/dataset-validators.test.ts` — null coords + `hasLocation: false` → valid; null coords without it → throw; half-null coords → throw) +2 React (CommandMap: no marker for a no-fix SOS, 1 marker for a located control; CentralCommand: clicking "تحديد" on a no-fix SOS sets no focus target — proven via a `recordedFocus` capture in the shared CommandMap mock — + located control focuses 36.75/3.06). SOS-6 +1 route (`tests/sos.test.ts` — dispatch once `"ok"`, dispatch again `"already_dispatched"` → 200 + `alreadyDispatched: true` + the ORIGINAL `dispatchedAt`; GET `/api/sos/:id` shows `dispatchedTeams` of length 1) + 2 UPDATED atomicity assertions (ARC-R4 re-dispatch tests `toBe("ok")` → `toBe("already_dispatched")`; durable assertions unchanged; the "genuine re-dispatch after clear" test still expects `"ok"` + 2 rows). TEAM-6 +2 (`tests/teams-operations.test.ts` — heartbeat with `fixTimeMs` → GET returns it; control heartbeat without it → `null`). USR-2 +2 (`tests/users-security.test.ts` — DELETE with one affected roster day and `docSet` → false → 200 + `rosterCleanup: {days: 1, removed: 0, failed: 1}`; control `docSet` → true → `{days: 1, removed: 1, failed: 0}`; the mocked `docSet` is reached via `import * as fsMockModule from "../server/fs.js"`). VOL-3 +1 (`tests/volunteers.test.ts` — register via POST, GET `/pending` → 1 item, delete the doc from `state.docs` directly, invalidate the 30 s collection cache, GET `/pending` → `[]`; the fake DB's `collection()` gained a chainable query — `orderBy` → `limit` → `get()` over `state.docs` sorted desc by the order field — without it `collectionGet` threw and `loadRegs` returned stale memory on BOTH old and new code).
- **Fail-without-fix (proven):** stashing the 9 source files fails exactly 12 checks — SOS-5 ×4 (feed, validator, marker, focus), SOS-6 ×1 route, TEAM-6 ×2 (finding + control — the control discriminates on the `undefined`-vs-`null` distinction, prep §6 note 5, named honestly), USR-2 ×2 (both — the `rosterCleanup` field itself is the fix, so the success-path test also fails on old code), VOL-3 ×1, + the 2 updated ARC-R4 assertions (they track the new outcome type — lesson (jj)). All other targeted tests pass (183/195 targeted run on old code).
- **Implementation incidents (parent-caught):** (1) Lint failed after the first implementation: widening `TrappedSOS.lat/lng` to `number | null` broke `L.marker([sos.lat, sos.lng])` — TypeScript does not narrow through a `.filter().forEach()` closure. Fixed with a type-predicate filter (`(s): s is TrappedSOS & { lat: number; lng: number } => ...`); lint clean after. (2) The VOL-3 test needed two harness fixes beyond the prep: the fake DB's chainable query (above) AND a collection-cache invalidation between the two GETs (the 30 s TTL would otherwise serve the cached list and the test would fail on new code too).

## Fix 43b — CodeRabbit round on PR #174 (4 Minors, all verified in code)

- **Watch 1 (head dfee6c5): DO NOT MERGE** — all own checks green (lint-and-test, build-and-test, e2e, release, Analyze ×2; CodeQL neutral; instrumented in progress — known pre-existing non-blocker); CodeRabbit review 6039588272 (2026-11-24T12:57:43Z): 4 actionable Minors, all four verified VALID by the watcher AND by the parent in code at head. All four are regressions/omissions Fix 43 itself introduced (the SOS-6 response shape and the USR-2 `rosterCleanup` field did not exist before Fix 43).
- **Minor 1 (inline 4680302601, `server/routes/sos.ts:613`)** — the `already_dispatched` branch returned the freshly built item after a process restart (`memorySos` lacks the SOS), with a fresh `dispatchedAt` and the retry's `notes` — data describing a write that never happened. The durable dispatch exists in the `trappedSos` doc (`appendSosDispatch` arrayUnions it). **Minor 4 (review body, same line)** — `.find()` returns the FIRST (oldest) entry, and entries accumulate (clear + re-dispatch appends a second entry for the same team; nothing ever removes one), so after clear + re-dispatch a repeat dispatch returned the SUPERSEDED dispatch's `dispatchedAt`/`status`/`notes`.
- **Minor 2 (inline 4680302612, `server/routes/users.ts:151`)** — when `collectionGet` returns `null` (read failure) the sweep never runs, no log is written, and the response still returns `{success: true, rosterCleanup: {days: 0, removed: 0, failed: 0}}` — zeros indistinguishable from "no affected days", so the deleted agent may silently remain on planned rosters. Fix 43's new `rosterCleanup` field is what makes the zeros an active (misleading) claim.
- **Minor 3 (review body, `src/components/CentralCommand.tsx:304`)** — `sendDispatchRequest` calls `applyLocalDispatch` on ANY `res.ok`, and `applyLocalDispatch` unconditionally appends — so an `already_dispatched` 200 appends a client-only phantom duplicate the server deliberately did not append, diverging from the server until the next SOS poll. (One premise in the comment is wrong — "previously, HTTP 409 skipped this branch": a same-team re-dispatch returned 200 before Fix 43 too — but the finding holds.)

CentralCommand.tsx — `applyLocalDispatch` upserts by `teamId` (replace the matching entry, add when absent; an item without a teamId — the raw request-body fallback — still appends). types.ts — `TrappedSOS.dispatchedTeams` items gain optional `teamId?: string` (the server has always sent it; the type just didn't declare it).
